# Graph Report - vega  (2026-11-19)

## Corpus Check
- 3295 files · ~5,495,247 words
- Verdict: corpus is large enough that graph structure adds value.
- Unclassified: 743 file(s) not represented in the graph (top: (none) 320, .scm 156, .wit 77)

## Summary
- 112550 nodes · 351105 edges · 2232 communities (1675 shown, 557 thin omitted)
- Extraction: 96% EXTRACTED · 4% INFERRED · 0% AMBIGUOUS · INFERRED: 13366 edges (avg confidence: 0.76)
- Token cost: 0 input · 0 output

## Graph Freshness
- Built from commit: `06a23d5c`
- Run `git rev-parse HEAD` and compare to check if the graph is stale.
- Run `graphify update .` after code changes (no API cost).
